- **large-scale-changes**: Applies a mechanical change across many files, packages, or owners with a script and per-owner shards. Use when writing or running a codemod, or when the user says migrate every call site, rename this everywhere, bump X across all packages, roll out this lint rule or config everywhere, or split this into PRs per owner. Skip changes that fit one reviewable PR and structural redesigns, which refactor-architecture owns.
